## Cuccaro et al. (2004) · [A new quantum ripple-carry addition circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/quant-ph/0410184)

- **Derived result**. Cuccaro majority/unmajority ripple carry lowered to actual Qatlib legal one-/two-qubit words, counting each Toffoli as 15. The k paired low bits plus outgoing carry cost at most 34k+1 primitive instructions. [circuit](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple.circuit) · [finish_action](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple.finish_action) · [prepare_action](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple.prepare_action)
- **Supporting lemma**. Disjoint wire addressing allows the unknown addend, carry, and outer wires of the recursive majority ladder to be tracked individually. [innerWires](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple.innerWires) · [member](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple.member) · [read_congr](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple.read_congr)
- **Special case**. Cuccaro addition with an arbitrary borrowed carry and high target. The low target plus weighted carry-out equals the two input low words plus the initial carry. The high target is XORed with the carry-out and all spectator bits are restored. [arithmetic](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple.arithmetic) · [restored](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.BorrowedRipple.restored)
- **Supporting lemma**. A legal phase-free Boolean gate word has an equal-length inverse. Local CNOTs implement a controlled bitwise complement, and little-endian concatenation and complementation have their ordinary modular arithmetic meanings. [controlledNotWord](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement.controlledNotWord) · [inverseWord](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement.inverseWord) · [modulus](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement.modulus) · [read_append](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement.read_append) · [read_complement](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement.read_complement) · [read_eq](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement.read_eq)
- **Supporting lemma**. The compiled ripple adds the preserved garbage addend and incoming carry modulo 2ᵏ. Conjugation by a conditional bitwise complement changes the sign of an increment without changing its magnitude. [adds](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement.adds) · [complement_value](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement.complement_value) · [projections](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement.projections) · [reflectedCarry](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement.reflectedCarry) · [tickCircuit](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement.tickCircuit)
- **Derived result**. Controlled or unconditional addition of one on k target wires using k borrowed wires, restoring their arbitrary contents. In the proved variant the inverse ripple and forward ripple see opposite input carries when enabled, so the unknown addend cancels. Counting actual one-/two-qubit gates gives ≤70k+2. [manyDirty](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.DirtyIncrement.manyDirty)
- **Derived result**. Gidney §2.10, implemented with a borrowed-carry ripple variant. Equal register halves exchange workspace and an odd width peels its high-order controlled flip. Any k distinct addressed wires can be incremented modulo 2ᵏ with one borrowed wire in ≤720(k+3) legal one-/two-qubit Clifford+T instructions. [evenHalves](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.HalvedIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.HalvedIncrement.evenHalves) · [evenWidth](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.HalvedIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.HalvedIncrement.evenWidth) · [oneDirty](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.HalvedIncrement/FQChemQatlib.Dynamics.HalvedIncrement.oneDirty)

## Di Marino, Lewin & Nenna (2024) · [Ground state energy is not always convex in the number of electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08632)

- **Literature hypothesis · needs Lean proof**. [Atomic energy convexity in electron number](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#paper/atomic-energy-convexity). Di Marino et al., Eq. (4) · Hofacker, Appendix C, Eq. (63). At fixed positive integer nuclear charge, twice the ground energy of the N-electron Coulomb atom should be at most the sum for N−1 and N+1 electrons. The variational definition includes both spin states and simultaneous fermionic exchange. The six-centre counterexample concerns a different external potential.
  - [Formal statement in Lean](https://github.com/the-matter-lab/fqchem/blob/6770a38071e5b621be99d9d04edbf8a8e41cbd6c/FQChem/Unproven/AtomicEnergy.lean#L50)

## Eyring (1935) · [The Activated Complex in Chemical Reactions](https://doi.org/10.1063/1.1749604)

- **Derived result**. For a shared positive rate prefactor, nonnegative inverse temperature β and a barrier error at most ε, the predicted/exact exponential rate ratio lies between exp(−βε) and exp(βε). [barrierRateError](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.Multiscale.StitchingSolutions.ErrorPropagation/FQChem.Multiscale.barrierRateError)

## Farhi et al. (1998) · [Limit on the Speed of Quantum Computation in Determining Parity](https://doi.org/10.1103/PhysRevLett.81.5442)

- **Supporting lemma**. The product of Boolean signs equals ±1 and is orthogonal under the cube sum to every polynomial of total degree strictly below the number of bits. [parity_cases](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.Complexity.OrbitalSimulation/FQChem.Complexity.OrbitalSimulation.parity_cases) · [parity_orthogonal](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.Complexity.OrbitalSimulation/FQChem.Complexity.OrbitalSimulation.parity_orthogonal)
- **Derived result**. In the library’s two-orbital model, the diagonal onsite energy is bounded by π. Evolving for time N implements a relative parity sign, which determines the antibonding population for N > 0. [evolution](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.Complexity.OrbitalSimulation/FQChem.Complexity.OrbitalSimulation.evolution) · [hermitian](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.Complexity.OrbitalSimulation/FQChem.Complexity.OrbitalSimulation.hermitian) · [onsite_bounds](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.Complexity.OrbitalSimulation/FQChem.Complexity.OrbitalSimulation.onsite_bounds) · [population_parity](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.Complexity.OrbitalSimulation/FQChem.Complexity.OrbitalSimulation.population_parity)
- **Derived result**. For individual Boolean potential-term queries, uniform additive error < 1/2 on the parity-encoding population at time N forces N ≤ 2T. Berry et al., Theorem 3, uses sparse matrix row queries and trace distance for a different Hamiltonian family. [simulation_query_lower_bound](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.Complexity.OrbitalSimulation/FQChem.Complexity.OrbitalSimulation.simulation_query_lower_bound)

## Feit, Fleck & Steiger (1982) · [Solution of the Schrödinger equation by a spectral method](https://doi.org/10.1016/0021-9991%2882%2990091-2)

- **Supporting lemma**. The symmetric product VTV has (VTV)†(VTV)=I when each factor does. This verifies norm preservation of a split step and leaves splitting error as a separate estimate. [splitOperator](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.Dynamics.RealTimePropagation.SplitOperator/FQChem.Dynamics.splitOperator)

## Feshbach (1958) · [Unified theory of nuclear reactions](https://doi.org/10.1016/0003-4916%2858%2990007-1)

- **Special case**. Assuming z−D invertible on the eliminated sector, a nonzero eigenvector of a block operator is equivalent to a nonzero solution of the effective equation with unique reconstructed environment component. [feshbachReduction](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.Multiscale.StitchingSolutions.EmbeddedSolvers/FQChem.Multiscale.Embedding.feshbachReduction)

## Feynman (1939) · [Forces in Molecules](https://doi.org/10.1103/PhysRev.56.340)

- **Derived result**. The derivative of a normalized differentiable eigenbranch of a locally symmetric bounded Hamiltonian supplies the high-level force in the one-coordinate ONIOM surface. [layeredEigenForce](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.Multiscale.StitchingSolutions.LayeredQM/FQChem.Multiscale.layeredEigenForce)
- **Derived result**. On a one-coordinate Newton trajectory driven by the eigenbranch force and both MM forces, kinetic plus layered electronic potential energy has zero derivative. [layeredBornOppenheimerEnergy](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.Multiscale.StitchingSolutions.LayeredQM/FQChem.Multiscale.layeredBornOppenheimerEnergy)
- **Special case**. For a differentiable eigenbranch in a real Hilbert space and bounded differentiable H, normalization and symmetry at the evaluation point give E′=⟨ψ,H′ψ⟩. [hellmannFeynman](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.Properties.ResponseAndEstimation.HellmannFeynman/FQChem.hellmannFeynman)

## Fock (1930) · [Näherungsmethode zur Lösung des quantenmechanischen Mehrkörperproblems](https://doi.org/10.1007/BF01340294)

- **Derived result**. A nonzero finite-basis Slater determinant is antisymmetric, so its Rayleigh quotient bounds the infimum over the fermion sector. The proof concerns the trial-state upper bound used in Hartree–Fock. [slaterTrialBound](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.StatePrep.VariationalOptimization.FermionicAnsatz/FQChem.VariationalMethods.slaterTrialBound)

## Galerkin (1915) · [Rods and plates: series expansions for problems of elastic equilibrium](https://www.unige.ch/~gander/HistoricalReferences/GALERKIN.pdf)

- **Special case**. For a bounded operator and an orthogonally projectable subspace, a nonzero trial vector solves the compressed eigenproblem exactly when its full residual is orthogonal to the trial subspace. [residualCriterion](https://fqchem.matterlab.ca/#FQChem.StatePrep.SubspaceDiagonalization.RitzGalerkin/FQChem.StatePrep.SubspaceDiagonalization.residualCriterion)
